Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The oceanography variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 892 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 148 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_892(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 892. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^148(S, F) is trivial. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the oceanography structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 764. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
